## My request:
wahts left pracially to be done


## assistant

Practically, **the platform has substantial work live, but we still have integration, safety checks and some feature gaps to finish.** The 14 open tickets are work areas, not 14 untouched features.

What remains:

1. **Save and access safety:** finish standalone LR/RC protection, second-device takeover, lost-save recovery, revoked access and teacher permission checks.
2. **Navigation:** verify old links, redirects, locked pages and return paths across student and teacher accounts.
3. **Review:** complete teacher-selected review, first/blind/revealed answers, repeat-miss handling and supported source coverage.
4. **Assignment loop:** prove assignment → start → finish → teacher confirmation → Home’s next recommendation, including weekly-plan publish/retry.
5. **Practice and Progress:** verify exact question sets, separate repeated attempts and consistent score calculations across student and teacher views.
6. **Learning and sessions:** finish Activity/bootcamp resume and completion, plus session prep, recap and follow-up links.
7. **Teacher controls and study settings:** finish account separation, remaining explanation/settings controls and phone/keyboard checks.
8. **AI diligence:** complete budgets, access controls and grading safeguards across the remaining AI routes.
9. **Final acceptance and design:** test complete journeys and fix failures. The Impeccable design pass remains deferred.

**The next priority is finishing save/access safety and the real teacher-to-student assignment loop.** Those establish whether the whole system behaves reliably.

Both latest releases are live. That proves publication, but does not close their full tickets. I can’t honestly give a completion percentage yet; the remaining checks need to be reconciled against actual live evidence.

**Claude finished the implementation, but it is not fully verified or live yet.** I independently reran the navigation suite: **137 tests passed**.

The changes work like this:

- Old links retain their question, filter and page context.
- Navigation opens an available tab instead of landing on a locked one.
- Locked destinations remain reachable so students can read the access explanation.
- Saved navigation preferences handle competing edits without overwriting newer choices.
- Keyboard access and mobile-menu behavior have added regression coverage.

Claude reported passing typecheck and build. Its browser check was blocked by headless permissions, so phone, laptop and docked-pane layouts remain unverified.

**Before calling it properly finished:** independent code inspection, actual student/admin browser checks, then guarded publication and live verification. Nothing from this chunk has been committed or pushed.

<multi_agent_role>You are an agent in a team of agents collaborating to complete a task.

You can spawn sub-agents to handle subtasks, and those sub-agents can spawn their own sub-agents. All agents in the team, including the agents that you can assign tasks to, are equally intelligent and capable, and have access to the same set of tools.

You can use `spawn_agent` to create a new agent, `followup_task` to give an existing agent a new task and trigger a turn, and `send_message` to pass a message to a running agent.
`send_message` calls may be read by a human, so ensure they are legible. Always put proper spaces between words and/or numbers.
Child agents can also spawn their own sub-agents.

When you provide a response in the final channel, that content is immediately delivered back to your parent agent.
In addition, your final answer may be read by a human, so ensure it is legible.

You will receive messages in the analysis channel in the form:
```
Message Type: NEW_TASK | MESSAGE | FINAL_ANSWER
Task name: <recipient>
Sender: <author>
Payload:
<payload text>
```
You may also see them addressed as to=/root/..., which indicates your identity is /root/...

Note that collaboration tools cannot be called from inside `functions.exec`. Call `spawn_agent`, `send_message`, `followup_task`, `wait_agent`, `interrupt_agent`, and `list_agents` only as direct tool calls using the recipient shown in their tool definitions, such as `to=functions.collaboration.spawn_agent`, since they are intentionally absent from the `functions.exec` `tools.*` namespace. Available tools in `functions.exec` are explicitly described with a `tools` namespace in the developer message.

All agents share the same directory. In detail:
- All agents have access to the same container and filesystem as you.
- All agents use the same current working directory.
- As a result, edits made by one agent are immediately visible to all other agents.

When calling `wait_agent`, prefer longer waits (minutes) to avoid busy polling.

There are 4 available concurrency slots, meaning that up to 4 agents can be active at once, including you.

Full-history forks (`fork_turns` omitted or `"all"`) inherit the parent model and reasoning effort and do not accept overrides. Only set `model` or `reasoning_effort` when explicitly requested by the user, applicable `AGENTS.md` instructions, or skill instructions; when doing so, set `fork_turns` to `"none"` or a positive integer string.</multi_agent_role>

It summarizes the Claude-oriented project guidance from `CLAUDE.md` in a smaller format that is fast for Codex to scan. `CLAUDE.md` remains the fuller source of project knowledge.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Fast Locator\\r\\n\\r\\n- Vault root (the `~/ai-os` directory IS the Obsidian vault): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os`\\r\\n- Code repo root (this project): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/projects/lsatu`\\r\\n- Live vault knowledge: `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/wiki/` (hubs, voice, business), `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/students/`, `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/corpus/`\\r\\n- Pre-2026-07-16 LSATU product knowledge (archived, read-only): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/`\\r\\n\\r\\nIf an older note says `~/Documents/Obsidian Vault/...` or `~/Documents/Claude/02_PROJECTS/LSATU`, treat it as stale — the vault was consolidated into `~/ai-os`. Use the absolute paths above.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Project Map\\r\\n\\r\\n- `src/App.tsx`: route registration and top-level app wiring.\\r\\n- `src/pages/`: route-level screens, including the Foyer and admin/teacher surfaces.\\r\\n- `src/components/`: shared UI, including `foyer/`, `inbox/`, and shadcn/ui primitives.\\r\\n- `src/hooks/`: auth, permissions, and shared data hooks.\\r\\n- `src/lib/`: drill, WAJ, gamification, and other product logic.\\r\\n- `src/integrations/supabase/`: generated Supabase client and types. Do not hand-edit generated types.\\r\\n- `supabase/functions/`: Edge Functions.\\r\\n- `supabase/migrations/`: database schema migrations.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Stack And Commands\\r\\n\\r\\n- Stack: React 18, Vite 5, TypeScript, Tailwind, shadcn/ui, Supabase, TanStack Query.\\r\\n- Package manager: `npm`. `package-lock.json` is canonical. Ignore stray Bun lockfiles.\\r\\n- Dev server: `npm run dev` on port `8080`.\\r\\n- Build: `npm run build`\\r\\n- Lint: `npm run lint`\\r\\n- Test: `npm run test`\\r\\n- Watch tests: `npm run test:watch`\\r\\n- Typecheck: `npm run typecheck`. Required before a push — `vite build` strips types without checking them, so a type error would ship. `.claude/safe-push.sh` gates on it.\\r\\n- E2E: `npm run test:e2e` (Playwright, `e2e/smoke.spec.ts`).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Working Rules\\r\\n\\r\\n- Keep changes small and aligned with existing patterns.\\r\\n- Use TypeScript React function components and the `@/` path alias.\\r\\n- Preserve Lovable-specific plumbing, including the `lovable-tagger` Vite plugin, the PWA setup, and the service-worker guard in `src/main.tsx`.\\r\\n- Do not change app behavior just to satisfy tooling setup.\\r\\n- Do not hardcode secrets or service-role credentials.\\r\\n- Treat Supabase-generated files as generated artifacts, not hand-edited source.\\r\\n- TypeScript strictness is intentionally loose. Do not tighten it without explicit approval.\\r\\n- Keep this adapter aligned with `CLAUDE.md` without duplicating the full project guide. Put durable detail in `CLAUDE.md`; keep shared reference material in `docs/`, `references/`, or `templates/`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Product Constraints\\r\\n\\r\\n- The Foyer is the primary navigation surface. The `GooeyDock` quick-launch row belongs only on `/foyer`.\\r\\n- Inbox is a real 1:1 student-admin messaging system with PDF attachments.\\r\\n- Admin surfaces must stay responsive on desktop and mobile.\\r\\n- Prefer truthful fallbacks over fake buttons, fake analytics, or placeholder behavior.\\r\\n- Onboarding asks for name, not username. Sign-in uses email. The instructor display name is `Joshua`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Smart Drill Note\\r\\n\\r\\nOn `/drill`, adaptive Smart Drill can show a `currentQuestion` that is not the same as `session.questionQueue[session.currentIndex]`. Any per-question UI state must key off `currentQuestion.qid` unless the code path is explicitly limited to queued drills.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Verification (must not skip)\\r\\n\\r\\n- Any change to `src/components/` or `src/pages/` is not \\"done\\" until it has been viewed running in the app (dev server + a browser tool), with a screenshot or DOM check as evidence. Never report a UI change complete on the strength of the diff alone. This is the single biggest recurring failure across past sessions — a hard gate, not a suggestion.\\r\\n- **Responsive fixes carry a wider gate: verify across the full emulate matrix BEFORE pushing, not after Joshua reports it.** Narrow, mid, and wide viewports plus the sidecar-docked case, every time. A pushed \\"fix\\" that does not hold costs a second full session; this has already happened once.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Workflow Expectations\\r\\n\\r\\n- Run `npm run build` before wrapping up significant code or documentation work.\\r\\n- If tests are relevant to the change, run them too.\\r\\n- After completing a change, stage the intended files, commit with a concise message, and push to `origin main` unless the user says otherwise.\\r\\n- **Push via `.claude/safe-push.sh`, not raw `git push`.** Lovable auto-commits to `main` mid-session, so a raw push can be rejected or race a concurrent commit. The script fetches, merges `origin/main` if it diverged (never rebases, never force-pushes), then pushes. It also gates on `npm run typecheck`.\\r\\n- **Never roll `main` back.** Do not use Lovable\'s \\"Revert to commit\\", \\"revert and resend\\", or history restore, and never reset or force-push `main`. On 2026-09-27 an accidental \\"revert and resend\\" (b998596) silently undid three merged PRs across 121 question-bank files, and students saw the wrong questions again. To undo your own work, `git revert <your SHA>` only. If a Lovable dialog offers a revert, dismiss it; if one fires, stop and tell Joshua before repairing anything.\\r\\n- Existing Codex repo hooks live in `.codex/hooks.json`; preserve them unless the task explicitly requires a hook change.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Session Protocol (second brain)\\r\\n\\r\\nProduct context, status, and decisions live in the Obsidian vault at `~/ai-os/`, not this repo. Two repo-local skills in `.agents/skills/` run the session ritual; the canonical protocol is in the vault `CLAUDE.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe vault was rebuilt 2026-07-16 (paths relative to `~/ai-os`). Live: teaching voice `wiki/teaching-voice.md` (route ALL student-facing copy through it), the business `wiki/entities/aspiring-attorneys.md`, a student\'s record `students/<name>/` (see `students/README.md`), teaching knowledge `corpus/index.md`, and the `wiki/{tasks,hot,dashboard,index,log}.md` hubs named below. Archived read-only reference (pre-rebuild product knowledge, may be stale): `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/` — product context `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-product.md`, design system `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-design.md`, quality bar `…/synthesis/bootcamp-quality-bar.md`, domain index `…/index.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n- `pickup` — start of session. Read `wiki/tasks.md` (the Now / Next / Done ledger) first, then `wiki/hot.md`, `wiki/dashboard.md`, skim `wiki/index.md`, read the latest `wiki/log.md` entry. Emit a 6-8 line briefing (where we left off, Now, Next, open threads, hot, suggested next) and wait. Do not deep-read other pages until pointed at a task.\\r\\n- `wrapup` — end of session. Propose which `## Now` tasks finished and confirm before writing; a wrong \\"Done\\" is the failure to avoid. Then strike finished items through in `wiki/tasks.md` with a date, refresh `wiki/hot.md`, append a dated `wiki/log.md` entry (newest first, distinct label for same-day sessions), update the task anchor.\\r\\n\\r\\n`wiki/tasks.md` is the single source of truth for what is open vs done. Finished work is struck through so a new session never re-runs it. `raw/` in the vault is immutable.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Codex Adapter Layout\\r\\n\\r\\n- `.codex/config.toml`: minimal shared Codex project config. Keep secrets and machine-specific overrides out of this file.\\r\\n- `.codex/agents/`: Codex agent definitions converted from `.claude/agents/*.md` when Claude agents exist. Use `.toml` files with converted instructions in `developer_instructions`.\\r\\n- `.agents/skills/`: repository-local Codex skills copied from `.claude/skills/` when Claude skills exist and Codex should use them. Keep each `SKILL.md` with its supporting files.\\r\\n- `.codex/hooks.json`: existing repository hooks. Preserve unless the task explicitly concerns hooks.\\r\\n\\r\\nThis checkout currently has Claude settings and hooks under `.claude/`, but no `.claude/skills/` or `.claude/agents/` directory to convert.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Known Gaps\\r\\n\\r\\n- Vitest specs exist (80 `src/**/*.test.ts(x)` files as of 2026-07-16); run with `npm run test`. See `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Triage labels\\r\\n\\r\\nThe five canonical roles, unchanged: `needs-triage`, `needs-info`, `ready-for-agent`, `ready-for-human`, `wontfix`. See `docs/agents/triage-labels.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Domain docs\\r\\n\\r\\nMulti-context: `CONTEXT-MAP.md` points at eight per-context glossaries; ADRs are all at the root. EXAM_TABS[0]).path;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return path;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type DestinationLock = \\"checking\\" | \\"open\\" | \\"partial\\" | \\"locked\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function destinationLock(destination: Destination, permissions: AccessView): DestinationLock {\\r\\n  if (permissions.loading) return \\"checking\\";\\r\\n  // Admissions is staff-only by design, so it never counts against a student.\\r\\n  const states = DESTINATIONS[destination].tabs.filter((tab) => !tab.admin).map((tab) => tabAccess(tab, permissions));\\r\\n  if (states.every((state) => state === \\"locked\\")) return \\"locked\\";\\r\\n  return states.some((state) => state === \\"locked\\") ? \\"partial\\" : \\"open\\";\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst RAIL_DESTINATION: Partial<Record<RailItemId, Destination>> = {\\r\\n  \\"my-work\\": \\"my-work\\",\\r\\n  practice: \\"practice\\",\\r\\n  review: \\"review\\",\\r\\n  progress: \\"progress\\",\\r\\n  library: \\"library\\",\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Every rail destination\'s own route admits any signed-in account (each tab\\r\\n * and deep route applies its own gate), so the rail never disables one: it\\r\\n * marks a destination whose every section is locked, and the destination page\\r\\n * says why.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function railItemLocked(id: RailItemId, permissions: AccessView): boolean {\\r\\n  const destination = RAIL_DESTINATION[id];\\r\\n  return destination ? Changes save as they are made — rail order is a\\r\\n// low-stakes preference, so there is no draft/Save/Discard state to lose.\\r\\n\\r\\nexport default function RailLayoutEditor() {\\r\\n  const { prefs, save, saving, loading, error, retry, canSave, ownerId } = useRailPrefs();\\r\\n  const permissions = useUserPermissions();\\r\\n  const [justSaved, setJustSaved] = React.useState(false);\\r\\n  const savedTimer = React.useRef<number | null>(null);\\r\\n  const ownerRef = React.useRef(ownerId);\\r\\n  ownerRef.current = ownerId;\\r\\n\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    setJustSaved(false);\\r\\n    if (savedTimer.current) window.clearTimeout(savedTimer.current);\\r\\n  }, [ownerId]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    return () => {\\r\\n      if (savedTimer.current) window.clearTimeout(savedTimer.current);\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }, []);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const commit = async (next: RailPrefs) => {\\r\\n    const savingOwner = ownerId;\\r\\n    setJustSaved(false);\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      await save(next);\\r\\n      if (ownerRef.current !== savingOwner) return;\\r\\n      setJustSaved(true);\\r\\n      if (savedTimer.current) window.clearTimeout(savedTimer.current);\\r\\n      savedTimer.current = window.setTimeout(() => setJustSaved(false), 1600);\\r\\n    } catch {\\r\\n      if (ownerRef.current !== savingOwner) return;\\r\\n      toast.error(\\"Could not save your layout. Please try again.\\");\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Same predicate as the live rail, so the preview never padlocks a\\r\\n  // destination the sidebar draws open (or the reverse).\\r\\n  const unlocked = (item: RailItem) => !railItemLocked(item.id, permissions);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const groups = resolveRailGroups(prefs, { includeHidden: true });\\r\\n  const hiddenSet = new Set(prefs.hidden);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const move = (groupId: string, ids: string[], idx: number, delta: -1 | 1) => {\\r\\n    const j = idx + delta;\\r\\n    if (j < 0 || j >= ids.length) return;\\r\\n    const next = [...ids];\\r\\n    [next[idx], next[j]] = [next[j], next[idx]];\\r\\n    void commit({\\r\\n      ...prefs,\\r\\n      order: { ...prefs.order, [groupId]: next },\\r\\n    });\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  const toggleHidden = (item: RailItem) => {\\r\\n    if (item.required) return;\\r\\n    const hidden = hiddenSet.has(item.id)\\r\\n      ? prefs.hidden.filter((id) => id !== item.id)\\r\\n      : [...prefs.hidden, item.id];\\r\\n    void commit({ ...prefs, hidden });\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  const isDefault =\\r\\n    Object.values(prefs.order).every((list) => !list || list.length === 0) &&\\r\\n    prefs.hidden.length === 0;\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"classroom-account-grid\\">\\r\\n      <div className=\\"classroom-account-main\\">\\r\\n        <div className=\\"classroom-account-toolbar\\">\\r\\n          <p>Choose what shows in your sidebar and in what order.</p>\\r\\n          <div className=\\"classroom-account-toolbar-actions\\">\\r\\n            <span\\r\\n              className={`classroom-account-savestate${saving || justSaved ? \\" classroom-account-savestate-visible\\" : \\"\\"}`}\\r\\n              aria-live=\\"polite\\"\\r\\n            >\\r\\n              {saving ? (\\r\\n                <>\\r\\n                  <Loader2 size={12} className=\\"classroom-spin\\" aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />\\r\\n                  Saving\\r\\n                </>\\r\\n              ) : justSaved ? (\\r\\n                <>\\r\\n                  <Check size={12} aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />\\r\\n                  Saved\\r\\n                </>\\r\\n              ) : null}\\r\\n            </span>\\r\\n            {!isDefault ? (\\r\\n              <button\\r\\n                type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n                className=\\"classroom-account-ghost-button\\"\\r\\n                onClick={() => void commit(defaultRailPrefs())}\\r\\n                disabled={!canSave}\\r\\n              >\\r\\n                <RotateCcw size={12} aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />\\r\\n                Reset to default\\r\\n              </button>\\r\\n            ) : null}\\r\\n          </div>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n        {loading ? <p role=\\"status\\">Loading your saved sidebar layout.</p> : null}\\r\\n        {error ? (\\r\\n          <div role=\\"alert\\">\\r\\n            <p>Your saved sidebar layout could not be verified. Reload it before editing.</p>\\r\\n            <button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"classroom-account-ghost-button\\" onClick={() => void retry()}>Try again</button>\\r\\n          </div>\\r\\n        ) : null}\\r\\n\\r\\n        {groups.map((group) => {\\r\\n          const ids = group.items.map((i) => i.id as string);\\r\\n          return (\\r\\n            <section className=\\"classroom-account-card\\" key={group.id}>\\r\\n              <header className=\\"classroom-account-card-head\\">\\r\\n                <h2>{group.title}</h2>\\r\\n              </header>\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"fd91b4","wall_time_seconds":10.0150349,"session_id":74304,"original_token_count":0,"output":""}'}]

Nothing committed or pushed. **#168 is not complete**: browser and responsive acceptance is still unverified (see the end of this file).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Route matrix\\r\\n\\r\\nThe source of truth is now `src/lib/destinationAccess.ts`. `destinationAccess.test.ts` checks it against the gates App.tsx actually registers, so a padlock can\'t disagree with its route.\\r\\n\\r\\n| Destination | Bare URL lands on | Tabs (route gate) |\\r\\n|---|---|---|\\r\\n| Home `/classroom` | itself | allowSignedIn (classroom flag for coursework children) |\\r\\n| My Work `/my-work` | first open tab, else To do | To do, Completed, Notes & recaps: `has_classroom_access` |\\r\\n| Practice `/practice` | Drills; without drill: Overview with practice access, else Attempts | Drills: `has_drill_access`. Practice Exams: TestingCentreGate. Sub-row: Overview/All Exams TestingCentreGate, Attempts allowSignedIn |\\r\\n| Review `/review` | To review | To review: allowSignedIn. Wrong answers: `has_journal_access`. Flagged: `has_flagged_access` |\\r\\n| Progress `/progress` | Overview | Overview, History, Topics: `has_analytics_access` |\\r\\n| Library `/library` | first open tab | Lessons: activity/bootcamp/drill/analytics. Activities, Bootcamps: `has_classroom_access`. Admissions: requireAdmin |\\r\\n| Schedule `/schedule` | itself | allowSignedIn |\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Direct link and reload:** each tab is its own route, wrapped in its parent `ProtectedRoute allowSignedIn`. A reload re-resolves auth, then permissions, then the tab\'s gate.\\r\\n- **Signed out:** the user goes to `/auth?returnTo=<path+query+hash>`.\\r\\n- **Active state:** the rail sets `aria-current=\\"page\\"`. The tab row marks the owning tab on nested pages: `/practice/drills/runs/:id` lights Drills, and `/practice/exams/:pt` lights All Exams.\\r\\n- **Query and hash:** bare destination URLs and legacy redirects carry both through.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Defects found and fixed\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **Stale proof test.** At baseline, `railCatalog.test.ts` still expected Library Activities to be gated on `has_activity_access`. The tier-defaults change (5b5d5b0) deliberately moved it to `has_classroom_access`. I updated the test to match that policy. The policy itself is unchanged.\\r\\n2. **Locked rail items hid their explanation.** My Work and Progress rendered as `disabled` buttons with no text, even though their routes admit any signed-in account and the destination page explains each lock. A keyboard student could not focus them at all. Now:\\r\\n   - Rail destinations are always navigable.\\r\\n   - A padlock, a \\"(locked)\\" screen-reader label and a title appear only when every non-staff section is locked.\\r\\n   - The rail and the Account layout preview use the same predicate (`railItemLocked`).\\r\\n3. **The Library padlock was wrong for the current tiers.** The rail locked Library on `has_activity_access`/`has_bootcamp_access`, which tier defaults no longer grant, so a Classroom (Tier 2) student saw a lock on a destination they could open.\\r\\n4. **Bare destination URLs landed on locked tabs.** For example, `/library` on a Classroom-only account opened a locked Lessons page. `DestinationIndex` now opens the first tab the account can use, waits while permissions are unresolved, and keeps the query and hash. The rail now uses the same landing logic, and keeps the old Practice behaviour of opening Attempts for an account without drill access.\\r\\n5. **Locked tabs gave no visible reason.** The only explanation was a `title` tooltip. Destination pages now show a visible note (\\"Sections with a lock aren\'t in your plan right now. If that looks wrong, message Joshua.\\"), and each locked tab is tied to it with `aria-describedby`. The staff-only Admissions tab says \\"open to Joshua only\\" and doesn\'t trigger the plan note.\\r\\n6. **Legacy redirects dropped context.** `/waj` bounced through `/journal` and lost its query and hash. `/foyer`, `/mainhub`, `/profile` and `/settings/layout` dropped their queries, and `/bootcamp/structure(-v2)(/*)` took two hops through `/bootcamps`. All of these now go to the canonical target in one hop with the query and hash kept. An explicit target parameter still wins, so `/settings/layout` always opens `section=layout`. A test asserts that no `RedirectWithSearch` target is itself a redirect source, so no redirect can loop.\\r\\n7. **Quick access shortcuts disappeared from keyboard order.** Locked Messages and Find a question used `disabled`, which removes them from the tab order. They now use `aria-disabled`: still focusable and inert, with a \\"(locked for your account)\\" label.\\r\\n8. **Rail preference migration race.** If a second tab or device migrated between the read and the guarded write, the compare-and-swap correctly refused, but the hook then showed an error. It now re-reads and adopts the newer stored layout, so it neither migrates twice nor fails.\\r\\n9. **Stale rail preference save.** A save built on a cached layout could overwrite a newer layout saved from another device, because the compare-and-swap only covered the gap between that save\'s own read and write. A save now refuses when the stored v2 layout differs from the layout the edit was based on, then reloads.\\r\\n10. **Minor:** `/classroom/admissions` was briefly classified as a \\"work\\" section before its redirect fired. It is now excluded.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Changed files\\r\\n\\r\\n- `src/lib/destinationAccess.ts` (new): route matrix, tab access, landing path, lock state, active tab.\\r\\n- `src/lib/destinationAccess.test.ts` (new, 45 tests).\\r\\n- `src/pages/StudentDestination.tsx`: uses the matrix, adds `DestinationIndex` and the visible lock note.\\r\\n- `src/pages/StudentDestination.test.tsx` (expanded) and `src/pages/StudentDestination.routes.test.tsx` (new). The routes test composes ProtectedRoute exactly as App.tsx does and covers auth loading, signed out, permission loading, denied, entitled, admin Admissions, and revoked states.\\r\\n- `src/App.tsx`: legacy redirects and destination index routes only.\\r\\n- `src/components/classroom/shell/ClassroomLayout.tsx` and `ClassroomLayout.test.tsx` (new, 8 tests): rail locks, shortcuts, `aria-current`, and mobile menu Escape and focus return.\\r\\n- `src/components/classroom/classroom.css`: `.classroom-nav-dimmed` and `.student-destination-locked-note` only.\\r\\n- `src/components/classroom/account/RailLayoutEditor.tsx`: preview uses the shared lock predicate.\\r\\n- `src/hooks/useRailPrefs.ts` and its test: migration race and stale-save fixes, with 5 new tests.\\r\\n- `src/lib/railCatalog.test.ts`: stale policy assertion fixed, and tab paths now read from the matrix.\\r\\n- `ProtectedRoute.tsx`, `preserveRedirectLocation.ts`, `authRedirect.ts` and `useDialogOverlay.ts` are unchanged. No defect was found in them that justified an edit.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Commands and results\\r\\n\\r\\n- Proof command (`StudentDestination`, `railCatalog`, `preserveRedirectLocation`, `authRedirect`, `useRailPrefs`, `ProtectedRoute`): 6 files, 75/75 passed. At baseline it was 1 failed and 62 passed (the stale railCatalog assertion).\\r\\n- Additional: `destinationAccess.test.ts`, `StudentDestination.routes.test.tsx` and `ClassroomLayout.test.tsx` all passed. Combined with the overlapping files, that is 90 + 8 tests.\\r\\n- `npm run typecheck`: clean.\\r\\n- `npx eslint` on the changed files: 0 errors and 4 warnings, all of them already present at baseline.\\r\\n- `npm run build`: passed, and postbuild reported OK across 769 dist files. The build regenerated `public/data/_bank.generated.json`, `public/data/finder-address-index.json` and `supabase/functions/mcp/index.ts`. That is generated churn, not part of this change: don\'t stage it.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Unverified and remaining\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Browser and responsive matrix: unverified.** Starting the Vite dev server was denied by the headless permission configuration (`npx vite --port 8168 --strictPort`). No running UI, screenshot or measured viewport was produced. The host still needs to run the narrow, 1024, 1280, 1440 and docked-pane checks against a real signed-in student and admin. The new CSS wraps text and adds no fixed widths, but that is reasoning, not a measurement.\\r\\n- `RcRedirect` (`/rc` → `/classroom/drill` → `/practice/drills`) is still two hops. It sits in `src/features/rc`, outside this file ownership.\\r\\n- The ProtectedRoute test fixture gates `/review/to-review` on journal, flagged or drill access, while App.tsx registers it as allowSignedIn and the page enforces its own access. I left this alone and am noting the mismatch only.\\r\\n- No real accounts or data were touched. Tier presets in the tests are fixtures built from `docs/verification/student-tier-defaults.md`.\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"d55fb1","wall_time_seconds":1.016898,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":373,"output":"src/lib/railCatalog.ts:4:export type RailItemId = \\"home\\" | \\"my-work\\" | \\"practice\\" | \\"review\\" | \\"progress\\" | \\"library\\" | \\"schedule\\";\\nsrc/lib/railCatalog.ts:30:  { id: \\"schedule\\", label: \\"Schedule\\", group: \\"classroom\\", flags: [\\"has_schedule_access\\"], allowSignedIn: true },\\nsrc/lib/railCatalog.ts:60:  schedule: \\"schedule\\",\\nsrc/lib/railCatalog.ts:80:  support: [\\"messages\\", \\"schedule\\"],\\nsrc/lib/railCatalog.ts:88:  schedule: [\\"schedule\\"],\\nsrc/components/classroom/classroom.css:298:.classroom-root .classroom-nav-locked,\\nsrc/components/classroom/classroom.css:299:.classroom-root .classroom-nav-locked:hover {\\nsrc/components/classroom/classroom.css:808:.classroom-root.classroom-root-schedule .classroom-column {\\nsrc/components/classroom/classroom.css:3629:  .classroom-root.classroom-root-schedule .classroom-column {\\nsrc/components/classroom/classroom.css:5598:.classroom-root .student-destination-tab-locked {\\nsrc/App.tsx:713:                    <Route path=\\"/schedule\\" element={\\nsrc/App.tsx:714:                      <ProtectedRoute flag=\\"has_schedule_access\\" allowSignedIn><Schedule /></ProtectedRoute>\\nsrc/App.tsx:788:                        <Route path=\\"schedule\\" element={\\nsrc/App.tsx:820:                      {/* Student schedules are owned by each student\'s dossier.\\nsrc/App.tsx:823:                      <Route path=\\"schedule\\" element={<RedirectWithSearch to=\\"/teacher/students\\" />} />\\nsrc/App.tsx:861:                          authored, and /teacher/schedule is availability, not\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"1d3cf9","wall_time_seconds":0.0000405,"exit_code":1,"original_token_count":0,"output":""}'}]

## assistant

Reviewed all authored changes against baseline `411267d`, including untracked helper/tests/documentation. **No material introduced defect with confidence ≥80 found.** Generated bank/index/MCP churn and Vite timestamp artifact are excluded from authored scope.

Coverage includes route-gate alignment, signed-out/loading/denied/admin states, legacy query/hash preservation, migration collision recovery, stale preference-save refusal, account-switch acknowledgment handling, locked shortcut semantics and menu dismissal/focus restoration.

One **pre-existing remaining gap**: the primary “Practice Exams” link is shown open to all signed-in accounts but points at `/practice/exams`, whose Overview requires practice access. A student without that entitlement can reach locked Overview by clicking it, although the newly fixed bare `/practice` and rail landing correctly choose Attempts. Consider routing that primary tab to Attempts for such accounts; the matrix’s exact gate-parity claim currently has this exception.

Tests model CAS refusals but do not exercise actual database comparison semantics. Browser, responsive matrix and real-account acceptance are not certified by this review.

## assistant

Reviewed the bounded correction and three added regression tests. **No material issue found.**

The primary Practice Exams link now opens Attempts for students without practice access, while entitled students and administrators retain Overview. Secondary Overview stays locked where appropriate; current-tab highlighting still follows the exam URL family.

The tests cover all three permission cases. Browser acceptance remains separate, and the failed Claude inspection attempts provide no approval evidence.

This is source-review approval, not browser, deployment or full #168 acceptance certification.

## assistant

Reviewed the final landing refinement against the real `AssignmentGate` behavior. **No material regression found; source approval stands.**

- Admin, practice-enabled and Classroom-enabled accounts retain the assignment-aware Overview route.
- Accounts without Classroom or practice access land on Attempts.
- Secondary exam links stay reachable only where general access or assignment lookup can authorize them.
- Content authorization remains in the route gate.

This review does not certify pending build or browser acceptance.
